  /* 本文专用：PPT 逐页切换（每个切换器自带 base 与页序，互不干扰） */
  .deck-viewer {
    background: #f4f6f4;
    border: 1px solid #e0e8e2;
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 16px 16px 12px;
    margin: 10px 0 22px;
    outline: none;
  }
  .deck-viewer:focus-visible { border-color: #9ec3b1; }
  .deck-stage {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #e3ebe6;
    border-radius: 10px;
    overflow: hidden;
    line-height: 0;
    max-height: 72vh;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .deck-stage .deck-slide {
    display: block;
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 72vh;
    object-fit: contain;
    height: auto;
    margin: 0;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: 0;
  }
  .deck-bar {
    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 10px 2px 2px;
  }
  .deck-nav {
    width: 32px;
    height: 32px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid #d7e2db;
    background: #fff;
    color: #2e7d53;
    font-size: 18px;
    line-height: 1;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .deck-nav:hover:not(:disabled) { background: #eef6f1; }
  .deck-nav:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }
  .deck-count {
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    font-size: 13px;
    color: #456052;
    min-width: 62px;
  }
  @media (max-width: 767px) {
    .deck-viewer { padding: 10px 10px 8px; }
  }
</style>

<script>
  // 一篇里可能有多个切换器：每个自己带 base + 页序，统一在这里初始化
  (function () {
    if (window.__trackingDeckInit) return;
    window.__trackingDeckInit = true;

    function setup(viewer) {
      var base = viewer.dataset.deckBase;
      var pages = JSON.parse(viewer.dataset.deckPages);
      var total = pages.length;
      var img = viewer.querySelector("[data-deck-img]");
      var countEl = viewer.querySelector("[data-deck-count]");
      var prev = viewer.querySelector("[data-deck-prev]");
      var next = viewer.querySelector("[data-deck-next]");
      var page = 0;

      function src(i) { return base + "/" + pages[i]; }

      function render() {
        img.src = src(page);
        countEl.textContent = (page + 1) + " / " + total;
        prev.disabled = page === 0;
        next.disabled = page === total - 1;
        if (page + 1 < total) { var pre = new Image(); pre.src = src(page + 1); }
      }

      prev.addEventListener("click", function () { if (page > 0) { page--; render(); } });
      next.addEventListener("click", function () { if (page < total - 1) { page++; render(); } });
      img.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        var r = img.getBoundingClientRect();
        var right = e.clientX - r.left > r.width / 2;
        if (right && page < total - 1) { page++; render(); }
        else if (!right && page > 0) { page--; render(); }
      });
      viewer.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
        if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && page > 0) { page--; render(); e.preventDefault(); }
        if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && page < total - 1) { page++; render(); e.preventDefault(); }
      });

      render();
    }

    function boot() {
      var all = document.querySelectorAll("[data-deck-viewer]");
      for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
        if (all[i].dataset.deckReady) continue;
        all[i].dataset.deckReady = "1";
        setup(all[i]);
      }
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
    } else {
      boot();
    }
    document.addEventListener("swup:contentReplaced", boot);
    document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", boot);
  })();
</script>

的）\ne_special_feature_name：e_专题名称（事件触发关联的）\ne_shop_id：e_店铺ID（事件触发关联的）\ne_shop_name：e_店铺名称（事件触发关联的）\ne_url_link：e_URL地址（事件触发关联的）\ne_event_correlation_component_id：e_事件关联组件ID（事件触发关联的）\ne_event_correlation_component_title：e_事件关联组件名称（事件触发关联的）","是","","否","SELECT\n d_大平台_商品详情页_SKU弹窗_确定_按钮_点击"],["v_大平台_和_赊销_确认订单页_提交订单_按钮_点击","v_bigplatform_and_stage_page_confirm_order_submit_button_click","统计老赊销和大平台的确认订单页提交订单按钮点击","虚拟事件","大平台、赊销","d_page_confirm_order_submit_button_click：d_确认订单页_提交订单_按钮_点击\n\nd_bigplatform_page_confirm_order_deposit_free_or_pay_now_button_click：d_大平台_确认订单页_去免押或立即支付_按钮_点击"]]}]}</script>

<style>
  /* 本文专用：埋点规范表 */
  .spec-lead { margin: 4px 0 10px; font-size: 14px; color: #6b7a72; }
  .spec-viewer {
    background: #f4f6f4;
    border: 1px solid #e0e8e2;
    border-radius: 12px;
    padding: 14px 14px 10px;
    margin: 10px 0 22px;
  }
  .spec-tabs { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 10px; }
  .spec-tab {
    border: 1px solid #d7e2db;
    background: #fff;
    color: #2f4a3e;
    font-size: 12.5px;
    line-height: 1;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
  .spec-tab:hover { border-color: #9ec3b1; }
  .spec-tab.is-active { background: #2e7d53; border-color: #2e7d53; color: #fff; }
  .spec-note {
    margin: 0 0 8px;
    font-size: 12.5px;
    color: #5c6b63;
  }
  .spec-note:empty { display: none; }
  .spec-scroll {
    background: #fff;
    border: 1px solid #e3ebe6;
    border-radius: 10px;
    overflow: auto;
    max-height: 78vh;
  }
  .spec-table {
    border-collapse: collapse;
    font-size: 12.5px;
    line-height: 1.5;
    width: 100%;
  }
  .spec-table th {
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 1;
    background: #3fa06d;
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-align: left;
    padding: 8px 10px;
    border: 1px solid #3fa06d;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .spec-table td {
    padding: 7px 10px;
    border: 1px solid #dce5de;
    vertical-align: top;
    background: #fff;
    color: #24312a;
    white-space: pre-wrap;
    min-width: 90px;
  }
  .spec-table tr:nth-child(even) td { background: #f7faf8; }
  .spec-hint { margin: 8px 2px 0; font-size: 12px; color: #93a29a; }
  @media (max-width: 767px) {
    .spec-viewer { padding: 10px 10px 8px; }
    .spec-table { font-size: 12px; }
  }
</style>

<script>
  (function () {
    if (window.__specViewerInit) return;
    window.__specViewerInit = true;

    function makeTable(rows) {
      var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      if (!rows.length) return frag;

      var thead = document.createElement("thead");
      var trh = document.createElement("tr");
      rows[0].forEach(function (cell) {
        var th = document.createElement("th");
        th.textContent = cell;
        trh.appendChild(th);
      });
      thead.appendChild(trh);
      frag.appendChild(thead);

      var tbody = document.createElement("tbody");
      for (var i = 1; i < rows.length; i++) {
        var tr = document.createElement("tr");
        rows[i].forEach(function (cell) {
          var td = document.createElement("td");
          td.textContent = cell;
          tr.appendChild(td);
        });
        tbody.appendChild(tr);
      }
      frag.appendChild(tbody);
      return frag;
    }

    function setup(viewer) {
      var holder = viewer.parentNode.querySelector("[data-spec-data]");
      if (!holder) return;
      var data = JSON.parse(holder.textContent);
      var sheets = data.sheets;

      var tabs = viewer.querySelector("[data-spec-tabs]");
      var table = viewer.querySelector("[data-spec-table]");
      var note = viewer.querySelector("[data-spec-note]");
      var hint = viewer.querySelector("[data-spec-hint]");

      function show(i) {
        var s = sheets[i];
        table.textContent = "";
        table.appendChild(makeTable(s.rows));
        note.textContent = s.note || "";
        hint.textContent = s.name + " · 共 " + Math.max(0, s.rows.length - 1) + " 行";
        var btns = tabs.querySelectorAll(".spec-tab");
        for (var k = 0; k < btns.length; k++) {
          btns[k].classList.toggle("is-active", k === i);
        }
      }

      sheets.forEach(function (s, i) {
        var b = document.createElement("button");
        b.type = "button";
        b.className = "spec-tab" + (i === 0 ? " is-active" : "");
        b.textContent = s.name;
        b.addEventListener("click", function () { show(i); });
        tabs.appendChild(b);
      });

      show(0);
    }

    function boot() {
      var all = document.querySelectorAll("[data-spec-viewer]");
      for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
        if (all[i].dataset.specReady) continue;
        all[i].dataset.specReady = "1";
        setup(all[i]);
      }
    }

    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
    } else {
      boot();
    }
    document.addEventListener("swup:contentReplaced", boot);
    document.addEventListener("astro:page-load", boot);
  })();
</script>
